# Experiment 3 report: soft books against Pinnacle at the same poll

Generated by `python -m nbatotals backtest-books --legacy` (maintainer only: needs the private multi-region payloads and legacy DB). Rules: [PREREGISTRATION.md](PREREGISTRATION.md). Test seasons: 2022-23, 2023-24, 2024-25. Exact-line pairing unless a table says converted. Threshold 2% unless stated. ROI and CLV intervals: 95% bootstrap over games, 10,000 resamples, fixed seed. Cover: 95% Wilson. CLV = Pinnacle's devigged true-close probability of the bet side at the bet's line minus p_fair at bet time. Line move = Pinnacle's true-close line relative to the bet's line, positive favouring the bet. Edge at close = p_close × price − 1. Exchanges are gross of commission.

## Lag: Pinnacle's previous move against each book's next move

corr_next = correlation between Pinnacle's move over b−1 → b and the book's move over b → b+1, pooled over the five bucket triples and test-season games (95% Fisher interval). Moves are line points (totals, spreads) or devigged home probability in cents (h2h). The Pinnacle row is Pinnacle's own correlation between consecutive moves; a book lags if its corr_next clearly exceeds that row. corr_same = correlation of the book's and Pinnacle's moves over the same b → b+1.
